# Gig: Spy in the Jungle

Journal: `ep1/quests/street_stories/sts_ep1_08` · `StreetStory` · hash `2847484989`.

Journal premise: Gig objective: Find Mark Bana\n\nLocation: Longshore Stacks\n\nDetails: Powerful fingers like to poke this little pie called Dogtown. But let's be honest witth ourselves... Without the spygames, without the power struggles between forces we scarcely understand, this place would become a bore like any other. Wouldn't you agree?\n\nTwo Brazilian intelligence agents recently reached out to me. One of their people has gone missing in Dogtown. The person in question is one Mark Bana.\n\nNaturally, as with all intelligence operations, there is a catch. Several, perhaps. I've identified one already. The agents seem to be acting without the approval of Brazilian Intelligence Agency. Might I advise inquiring as to their true motives.\n\nHave fun.

**Coverage: journal-linked.** 86 exact journal-linked nodes in 6 resources; 10 resources in the inspected dependency context.

## Required acceptance cases before reuse

Exercise normal completion and each named alternate outcome; interrupt and return at every scene handoff; start with spatial/item/actor predicates already satisfied; save/load across waits and joins; cancel parallel listeners; verify reward delivery once; and verify cleanup without changing the committed outcome. Apply the block-specific cases in the [expanded contracts](../all-quest-blocks.md). No installed playtest is claimed.
